Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦单轮受限场景，无法捕捉真实交互式科学工作流中LLM的能力边界，缺乏对科学知识组合泛化能力的系统评估。

Method: 形式化定义了组合顺序与混合结构，构建了包含20个领域、4类任务、8,598个交互会话的XDomainBench基准，设计了8种真实轨迹模式以模拟AI4S场景，从单学科到跨学科进行系统压力测试。

Result: LLM存在系统性推理崩溃：随着组合顺序增加，推理能力显著下降。两个根本原因：(i) 领域组合直接增加难度；(ii) 交互轨迹模式间接引发错误累积、推理断裂和领域混淆，最终导致会话完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在跨学科组合推理中存在严重能力瓶颈，尤其在高阶组合交互场景下表现脆弱，需要针对性改进。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for knowledge synthesis, yet their capacity for compositional generalization in scientific knowledge remains under-characterized. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on single-turn restricted scenarios, failing to capture the capability boundaries exposed by real-world interactive scientific workflows. To address this, we introduce XDomainBench, a diagnostic benchmark for interactive interdisciplinary scientific reasoning. We formalize the composition order and mixture structure to enable systematic stress-testing from single-discipline to inter-disciplinary, comprising 8,598 interactive sessions across 20 domains and 4 task categories, with 8 realistic trajectory patterns covering difficulty and domain-mixture dynamics, simulating real AI4S scenarios. Large-scale evaluation of LLMs reveals a systematic reasoning collapse as composition order increases, stemming from two root causes: (i) direct difficulty increases induced by domain composition, and (ii) indirect interaction-amplified failures where trajectory patterns trigger error accumulation, reasoning breaks, and domain confusion, ultimately leading to session collapse.

</details>


### [109] [Probabilistic Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks for Single and Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14758)
*Luca Marzari,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RNN-ProVe框架，通过策略驱动采样和统计误差界，对部分可观测RL中RNN策略的不期望行为进行带概率保证的估计，比现有工具更定量化且可扩展到多智能体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNN验证工具依赖受限的建模假设或粗粒度过近似，导致结果过于保守或无法得出结论，亟需一种既能处理RNN隐状态动态、又能提供可行概率保证的验证方法。

Method: 采用策略驱动采样来近似训练策略下可达的隐状态集合，并推导统计误差界，从而产生有界误差、高置信度的行为违规概率估计。

Result: 在部分可观测的单智能体和合作多智能体任务上，RNN-ProVe比现有工具提供更定量、更可行感知的概率保证，并能扩展到循环和多智能体设置。

Conclusion: RNN-ProVe有效解决了RNN策略在部分可观测RL中的验证难题，通过概率性框架提供了兼具量化精度和可扩展性的验证方案。

Abstract: History-dependent policies induced by recurrent neural networks (RNNs) rely on latent hidden state dynamics, making verification in partially observable reinforcement learning (RL) challenging. Existing RNN verification tools typically rely on restrictive modeling assumptions or coarse over-approximations of the hidden state space, which can lead to overly conservative or inconclusive results. We propose $\textbf{RNN}$ $\textbf{Pro}$babilistic $\textbf{Ve}$rification ($\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$), a probabilistic framework that $\textit{estimates the likelihood}$ of undesired behaviors in RNN-based policies. $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ uses policy-driven sampling to approximate the set of hidden states that are feasible under a trained policy, and derives statistical error bounds to produce bounded-error, high-confidence estimates of behavioral violations. Experiments on partially observable single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks show that $\texttt{RNN-ProVe}$ yields more quantitative, feasibility-aware probabilistic guarantees than existing tools, while scaling to recurrent and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [110] [MediaClaw: Multimodal Intelligent-Agent Platform Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14771)
*Shaoan Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Qiang Hui,Ting Lu,Xueqiang Guo,Yantao Li,Xinpei Su,Fuyuan Shi,Chao Tan,Fang Zhao,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediaClaw是一个基于OpenClaw生态的多模态智能体平台，采用三层架构（统一抽象层、插件化扩展层、工作流编排层），将AIGC能力统一抽象为调用模型，通过插件支持热插拔扩能，利用任务导向型Skill将复杂生产流程转化为可复用工作流资产，旨在解决AIGC落地中的能力碎片化、接口异构、流程割裂和优质工作流复用难等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AIGC实际部署中的痛点：能力碎片化、接口异构、生产流程割裂、高质量生产工作流复用受限。

Method: 采用三层架构设计：统一抽象层（将全品类AIGC能力抽象为统一调用模型）、插件化扩展层（支持热插拔能力扩展）、工作流编排层（通过任务导向型Skill将复杂生产流程转化为可复用工作流资产）。

Result: 构建了MediaClaw多模态智能体平台，实现了AIGC能力的统一抽象、插件化扩展和工作流编排复用。

Conclusion: 为构建多模态能力平台提供了可复用的实践参考，尤其在架构设计哲学、核心能力模型设计逻辑和工程实现权衡方面具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: MediaClaw is a multimodal agent platform built on the OpenClaw ecosystem. Its core design follows a three-layer architecture of unified abstraction, pluginized extension, and workflow orchestration. The system is intended to address practical deployment pain points in AIGC adoption, including fragmented capabilities, heterogeneous interfaces, disconnected production processes, and limited reuse of high-quality production workflows. \system{} abstracts full-category AIGC capabilities into a unified invocation model, uses plugins to support hot-pluggable capability expansion, and uses task-oriented Skills to turn complex production processes into reusable workflow assets. This report focuses on the architectural design philosophy of MediaClaw, the design logic of its core capability model, and the key engineering trade-offs in implementation. It aims to provide reusable practical reference for building multimodal capability platforms.

</details>


### [111] [Identifying Culprits Through Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient Deep Learning Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14774)
*Lata B T,Savitha N J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）的深度学习方法，用于从犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人档案等复杂数据集中高效识别罪犯，在降低误报和漏报的同时达到95%的准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的刑事调查依赖有限的数据分析，无法有效处理复杂数据集，且存在较高的误报率和漏报率，亟需一种更优的方法来准确识别罪犯。

Method: 采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）强化学习算法，使用犯罪现场材料、证人陈述和嫌疑人档案数据集进行训练，通过特征提取最大化识别罪犯的可能性，同时最小化噪声和无关数据的干扰。

Result: DDPG方法在罪犯识别任务上达到了95%的准确率，显著优于现有的多种方法。

Conclusion: 基于DDPG的深度学习方法能有效从复杂数据集中识别罪犯，降低误报和漏报，为刑事调查提供了高效的新方案。

Abstract: In the world of AI and advanced technologies investigation aspects identification of a crime or criminal plays a major problem. In this research we focus on a Conventional ways of implicating criminal investigations usually rely on limited data analysis. Finding an optimal and efficient method that will effectively identify criminals from complex datasets and minimise false positives and false negatives is the considered as a challenge. The main novelty approach of this work is based on the deep learning algorithm Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) is presented in this paper. We train the DDPG model with a dataset of crime scene material, witness statements and suspect profiles. The algorithm uses features to maximise the likelihood of identifying the offender while minimising the noise impact and irrelevant data. We show the efficacy of the proposed method, where DDPG identified criminals with an amazing accuracy of 95% than other several existing methods.

</details>


### [112] [A Heterogeneous Temporal Memory Governance Framework for Long-Term LLM Persona Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14802)
*Zhao Yang,Wang Huan,Li Yingshuo,Tu Haomiao,Lin Hujite*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARPM是一个外部时序记忆治理框架，通过分离静态知识与动态对话经验、结合向量检索与BM25等多重机制，将长程对话中的事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移等问题转化为可追踪、可审计、可转移的治理问题。实验表明，在50轮QA、高噪声基底（510万字）和跨模型切换等场景下，ARPM能维持语义连续性、边界连续性和人格一致性，但自动规则可能低估召回率，纯语义检索不足以支撑纠错与追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长程交互中常出现事实丢失、时间线混乱、人格漂移和稳定性下降等问题，尤其在高噪声知识库、上下文清除和跨模型迁移等场景下更为严重。现有方法要么将人格一致性编码进模型权重，要么仅依赖长上下文，缺乏可追溯、可审计的治理手段。

Method: ARPM框架包含：静态知识记忆与动态对话经验记忆的分离存储；向量检索、BM25、RRF融合、双时序重排序、时序证据阅读的结合；以及受控分析协议用于证据验证和答案绑定。通过工程日志进行三类实验：不同信噪比下的召回率对比、消融实验、高噪声+上下文清除+多模型切换下的鲁棒性测试。

Result: （1）信噪比1:5时CSV自动召回率54.0%，人工审查提升至100.0%；1:200+时分别为44.0%和80.0%。（2）消融实验：禁用对话历史检索时严格准确率从100%降至66.7%，禁用BM25降至80.0%。（3）在510万字噪声基底、上下文清除和多模型切换下，ARPM保持语义、边界和人格一致性，但暴露了弱协议遵从性导致的局限。

Conclusion: 长程人格一致性可被分解为可治理的组件，并以白盒方式进行评估。ARPM将连续性视为一个可追踪、可审计、可转移的治理问题，优于仅依赖模型权重编码或长上下文的方案。

Abstract: Large language models often suffer from fact loss, timeline confusion, persona drift, and reduced stability during long-range interaction, especially under high-noise knowledge bases, context clearing, and cross-model transfer. To address these issues, we introduce ARPM, an external temporal memory governance framework for long-term dialogue. ARPM separates static knowledge memory from dynamic dialogue experience memory and combines vector retrieval, BM25, RRF fusion, dual-temporal reranking, chronological evidence reading, and a controlled analysis protocol for evidence verification and answer binding. Unlike approaches that encode persona consistency into model weights or rely only on long context, ARPM treats continuity as a traceable, auditable, and transferable governance problem. Using engineering logs, we conduct three experiments. First, in a 50-round question-answering setting, we compare signal-to-noise ratios of 1:5 and 1:200+, and distinguish CSV auto-judgment from manual review. Under 1:5, CSV recall accuracy is 54.0%, while manual review raises it to 100.0%. Under 1:200+, the values are 44.0% and 80.0%. These results show that automatic rules can underestimate recall after supporting evidence enters the prompt. Second, ablation results show that dialogue history retrieval is necessary for recent continuity: disabling it reduces strict accuracy from 100% to 66.7%, and disabling BM25 reduces it to 80.0%, indicating that pure semantic retrieval is insufficient for correction and tracing. Third, under a 5.1-million-character noise substrate, periodic context clearing, and multi-model handoff, ARPM maintains semantic continuity, boundary continuity, and persona consistency, while exposing limits caused by weak protocol compliance. These findings show that long-term persona consistency can be decomposed into governable components and evaluated in a white-box manner.

</details>


### [113] [Interestingness as an Inductive Heuristic for Future Compression Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14831)
*Vincent Herrmann,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了“趣味性”（interestingness）概念，将其定义为未来压缩进展的归纳启发式，并利用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度和算法统计理论证明了其可预测性。研究表明，过去突破的近期性指数级地决定未来预期进展，且算法先验比长度先验更乐观，带来二次方增长预期发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决递归自我改进系统中的瓶颈之一——趣味性问题，即前瞻性地识别哪些任务或数据具有未来进步潜力的能力。

Method: 形式化趣味性为未来压缩进展的归纳启发式，利用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度和算法统计理论分析复杂度-运行时间分布，在长度先验、算法先验和速度先验下进行理论证明，并通过三种通用计算范式进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了期望未来进展依赖于最近一次观察到的突破的近期性（指数关系）；算法先验比长度先验更乐观，带来二次方增长预期发现；实验结果在三种通用计算范式上得到验证。

Conclusion: 趣味性的归纳性质（过去进展预示未来发现）在理论上成立且实验上可验证，为递归自我改进系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: One of the bottlenecks on the way towards recursively self-improving systems is the challenge of interestingness: the ability to prospectively identify which tasks or data hold the potential for future progress. We formalize interestingness as an inductive heuristic for future compression progress and investigate its predictability using tools from Kolmogorov Complexity and Algorithmic Statistics. By analyzing complexity-runtime profiles under Length, Algorithmic, and Speed priors, we demonstrate that the inductive property of interestingness -- the capacity for past progress to signal future discovery -- is theoretically viable and empirically supported. We prove that expected future progress depends exponentially on the recency of the last observed breakthrough. Furthermore, we show that the Algorithmic Prior is significantly more optimistic than the Length Prior, yielding a quadratic increase in expected discovery for the same observed profile. These findings are experimentally confirmed across three diverse universal computational paradigms.

</details>


### [114] [Holistic Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14865)
*Netta Madvil,Gilad Dym,Alon Mecilati,Edo Dekel,Jonatan Liberman,Rotem Brazilay,Liron Schliesser,Max Svidlo,Shai Nir,Orel Shalom,Yaron Friedman,David Connack,Amos Rimon,Philip Tannor,Shir Chorev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合自上而下的智能体级诊断与自下而上的跨度级评估的全方位智能体评估框架，通过将分析分解为独立于每个跨度的评估，实现对任意长度轨迹的规模化分析，并在TRAIL基准上取得了SOTA结果，相对提升最高达38%（类别F1）、3.5倍（定位准确率）和12.5倍（联合定位-分类准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体评估存在不足：结果指标只报告成功或失败而不解释原因，过程级方法难以将失败类型与其在长结构化轨迹中的精确位置关联起来。

Method: 提出一个全方位评估框架，包含自上而下的智能体级诊断和自下而上的跨度级评估，将分析分解为独立于每个跨度的评估，从而扩展到任意长度的轨迹并生成每个跨度的判断理由。

Result: 在TRAIL基准上，GAIA和SWE-Bench的所有指标均达到SOTA，相对提升：类别F1达38%、定位准确率达3.5倍、联合定位-分类准确率达12.5倍；每类别分析显示该框架在更多错误类别上领先；同一前沿模型在该框架内比作为整体判断时定位准确率高出数倍。

Conclusion: 评估方法论而非模型能力是当前AI智能体评估的瓶颈所在。

Abstract: AI agents execute complex multi-step processes, but current evaluation falls short: outcome metrics report success or failure without explaining why, and process-level approaches struggle to connect failure types to their precise locations within long, structured traces. We present a holistic agent evaluation framework that pairs top-down agent-level diagnosis with bottom-up span-level evaluation, decomposing analysis into independent per-span assessments. This decomposition scales to traces of arbitrary length and produces span-level rationales for each verdict. On the TRAIL benchmark, our framework achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on both GAIA and SWE-Bench, with relative gains over the strongest prior baselines of up to 38% on category F1, up to 3.5x on localization accuracy, and up to 12.5x on joint localization-categorization accuracy. Per-category analysis shows our framework leading in more error categories than any other evaluator. Notably, the same frontier model achieves several times higher localization accuracy when used inside our framework than as a monolithic judge over the full trace, showing that evaluation methodology, not model capability, is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [115] [Emotion-Attended Stateful Memory (EASM):The Architecture for Hyper-Personalization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14833)
*Vineet Kotecha,Vansh Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种情感注意力的状态记忆架构，通过长程历史、情感信号和意图推断动态构建用户特定上下文。在30场非脚本对话的A/B测试中，该架构相比无状态基线在记忆锚定（提升95%）、计划清晰度（57%）和情感验证（34%）上表现优异，即使在悲伤、痛苦等情感对抗场景中依然稳定。研究表明情感状态记忆可作为超个性化AI系统的基础设施层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型系统在会话间保持无状态，无法实现跨会话的个性化交互。尽管RAG和微调提升了知识获取与领域能力，但它们未能使模型具备对单个用户的持久理解能力。

Method: 提出情感注意力的状态记忆架构（emotion-attended stateful memory architecture），在推理时利用长程历史、情感信号和推断意图动态构建用户特定的会话上下文。采用同一底层语言模型，在六类情感类别的30场非脚本对话中进行A/B对照实验。

Result: 记忆增强条件在所有评估场景中一致优于无状态基线。最大提升体现在：记忆锚定（95%）、计划清晰度（57%）、情感验证（34%）。即使在悲伤、痛苦和不确定性等情感对抗对话中，结果依然保持一致性。

Conclusion: 状态化情感记忆可能代表超个性化AI系统的基础设施层，但需要在更大规模和更多样化的评估中进行更广泛的验证。

Abstract: Current language model systems remain fundamentally stateless across sessions, limiting their ability to personalize interactions over time. While retrieval-augmented generation and fine-tuning improve knowledge access and domain capability, they do not enable persistent understanding of individual users. We propose an emotion-attended stateful memory architecture that dynamically constructs user-specific conversational context using long-term history, emotional signals, and inferred intent at inference time. To evaluate its impact, we conducted a controlled A/B study across thirty non-scripted conversations spanning six emotionally distinct categories using the same underlying language model in both conditions. The memory-enriched condition consistently outperformed the stateless baseline across all evaluated scenarios. The largest gains were observed in memory grounding (95% improvement), plan clarity (57%), and emotional validation (34%). Results remained consistent even in emotionally adversarial conversations involving grief, distress, and uncertainty. These findings suggest that stateful emotional memory may represent a foundational infrastructure layer for hyper-personalized AI systems, though broader validation across larger and more diverse evaluations remains necessary

</details>


### [116] [Explainable Detection of Depression Status Shifts from User Digital Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14995)
*Loris Belcastro,Francesco Gervino,Fabrizio Marozzo,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释框架，结合多个BERT模型提取用户数字轨迹中的情感、情绪和抑郁严重度等多维信号，通过时间聚合和变化点检测分析抑郁相关状态转变，并集成大语言模型生成可读报告。在社交媒体数据集上评估表明，该方法比直接使用LLM报告具有更高的覆盖率、时间连贯性和变化点敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户每天产生的带有时间戳的数字痕迹（如社交媒体帖子、聊天记录）可能反映其心理状态，但这些轨迹中抑郁相关信号的演化模式（改善、恶化、稳定）缺乏有效的可解释分析方法。

Method: 提出一个可解释框架：1) 使用多个BERT模型提取情感、情绪、抑郁严重度等多维信号；2) 对时间序列信号进行聚合构建用户级轨迹；3) 分析轨迹以识别有意义的变点；4) 集成大语言模型生成描述心理信号演化和关键转变的简洁可读报告。

Result: 在两个社交媒体数据集上，该方法比直接基于LLM的报告生成产生更连贯且信息量更大的摘要，在用户历史覆盖率、时间连贯性和变点敏感性方面均更优。消融实验验证了各组件（尤其是时间建模和分段）的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了心理信号随时间演化的可解释视图，支持研究和决策，但不旨在进行临床诊断。

Abstract: Every day, users generate digital traces (e.g., social media posts, chats, and online interactions) that are inherently timestamped and may reflect aspects of their mental state. These traces can be organized into temporal trajectories that capture how a user's mental health signals evolve, including phases of improvement, deterioration, or stability. In this work, we propose an explainable framework for detecting and analyzing depression-related status shifts in user digital traces. The approach combines multiple BERT-based models to extract complementary signals across different dimensions (e.g., sentiment, emotion, and depression severity). Such signals are then aggregated over time to construct user-level trajectories that are analyzed to identify meaningful change points. To enhance interpretability, the framework integrates a large language model to generate concise and human-readable reports that describe the evolution of mental-health signals and highlight key transitions. We evaluate the framework on two social media datasets. Results show that the approach produces more coherent and informative summaries than direct LLM-based reporting, achieving higher coverage of user history, stronger temporal coherence, and improved sensitivity to change points. An ablation study confirms the contribution of each component, particularly temporal modeling and segmentation. Overall, the method provides an interpretable view of mental health signals over time, supporting research and decision making without aiming at clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [117] [A Deterministic Agentic Workflow for HS Tariff Classification: Multi-Dimensional Rule Reasoning with Interpretable Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14857)
*Yu Zhang,Dongjiang Zhuang,Qu Zhou,Zheng Huang,Junhe Wu,Jing Cao,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性智能体工作流（而非自规划智能体），用于HS海关编码分类任务。该工作流将控制流固定、LLM调用限制在狭窄阶段，通过离线知识工程与在线六阶段流水线结合，在HSCodeComp上达到64.2%（六位）和75.0%（四位）的Top-1准确率。人工审计发现部分真实标签可能偏离HS总规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HS关税分类是高风险的专家任务，需要多维度规则推理（材料、形态、功能、本质特征、部分与整体边界、具体列名与残余编码），端到端LLM提示在解决一个维度时会忽略其他维度的优先级约束，因此需要可解释且严格遵循规则的确定性方法。

Method: 采用**确定性智能体工作流**：控制流固定不可变，LLM调用仅限于狭窄的阶段步骤，反射和验证作为局部机制保留。架构结合离线中国HS税则知识工程与在线六阶段流水线，每个决策分解为阶段式结构化输出，并逐字引用相关章节注释。

Result: 在HSCodeComp上：四位编码Top-1 75.0%、Top-3 91.5%；六位编码Top-1 64.2%、Top-3 78.3%（Qwen3.6-plus）。开源Qwen3.6-27B-FP8非思考模式与前沿模型四位一致性84.2%、六位一致性77.4%。对226个六位分歧的两阶段人工审计表明，部分HSCodeComp真实标签可能偏离HS总规则。

Conclusion: 确定性工作流在HS分类中优于端到端LLM提示，其固有的可解释性和规则遵循能力是关键优势。部分公开数据集的标签质量存在可改进之处，全文附录发布了完整的裁决记录供社区审查。

Abstract: Harmonized System (HS) tariff classification is a high-stakes, expert-level task in which a free-form product description must be mapped to a specific six- or eight-digit code under the General Interpretive Rules (GIR), section notes, chapter notes, and Explanatory Notes. The difficulty lies not in knowledge volume but in *multi-dimensional rule reasoning*: a correct classification must satisfy competing priority rules along several axes simultaneously, including material, form, function, essential character, the part-versus-whole boundary, and specific listing versus residual headings. End-to-end prompting of large language models fails characteristically by resolving one axis while ignoring the priority constraints on the others. We present a *deterministic agentic workflow* in contrast to self-planning agents: the control flow is fixed, language model calls are confined to narrow stages, and reflection and verification are retained as local mechanisms. This design yields interpretability by construction--each decision is decomposed into stage-wise structured outputs with verbatim citation of the chapter or section notes that bear on it. The architecture combines offline knowledge-engineering of the Chinese HS tariff with an online six-stage pipeline. Evaluated on HSCodeComp at the six-digit level, the workflow reaches 75.0% top-1 and 91.5% top-3 at four digits, and 64.2% top-1 and 78.3% top-3 at six digits with Qwen3.6-plus; an open-weight Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 backbone in non-thinking mode achieves 84.2% four-digit and 77.4% six-digit top-1 agreement with the frontier model. A two-stage manual audit of 226 six-digit disagreements suggests that a non-trivial fraction of HSCodeComp ground-truth labels may deviate from HS general rules; full adjudication records are released in the appendix as preliminary findings for community review.

</details>


### [118] [Small, Private Language Models as Teammates for Educational Assessment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15015)
*Chris Davis Jaldi,Anmol Saini,Shan Zhang,Noah Schroeder,Cogan Shimizu,Eleni Ilkou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了大语言模型（LLMs）和小语言模型（SLMs）在评估题目设计任务上的表现，发现SLMs在关键教学质量维度上表现竞争性，且支持本地隐私部署，但模型评估与专家评价相比存在系统性的不一致性和偏差，因此提出语言模型应作为有边界的助手，并强调人机协同（Human-in-the-Loop）的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖主观或有限的评估方法，主要关注专有模型，缺乏对真实教育场景中生成、评估和部署约束的系统性考察；同时小语言模型在评估任务上的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 系统比较LLMs和SLMs在评估题目设计中的表现；使用可复现的、基于教学法的指标评估Bloom认知分类各层级的生成质量；分析基于模型的评判与专家评估之间的一致性和偏差模式。

Result: SLMs在关键教学质量维度上取得了与LLMs竞争性的表现，同时支持本地隐私敏感部署；但模型评估相对于专家评分存在系统性的不一致性和偏差。

Conclusion: 语言模型应被视为评估工作流中的有边界助手，必须引入人机协同（Human-in-the-Loop）机制；本研究通过考察质量、可靠性和部署权衡，推动了自动化教育题目生成领域的发展。

Abstract: Generative AI increasingly supports educational design tasks, e.g., through Large Language Models (LLMs), demonstrating the capability to design assessment questions that are aligned with pedagogical frameworks (e.g., Bloom's taxonomy). However, they often rely on subjective or limited evaluation methods; focus primarily on proprietary models; or rarely systematically examine generation, evaluation, or deployment constraints in real educational settings. Meanwhile, Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as local alternatives that better address privacy and resource limitations; yet their effectiveness for assessment tasks remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically compare LLMs and SLMs for assessment question design; evaluate generation quality across Bloom's taxonomy levels using reproducible, pedagogically grounded metrics; and further assess model-based judging against expert-informed evaluation by analyzing reliability and agreement patterns. Results show that SLMs achieve competitive performance across key pedagogically motivated quality dimensions while enabling local, privacy-sensitive deployment. However, model-based evaluations also exhibit systematic inconsistencies and bias relative to expert ratings. These findings provide evidence to posit language models as bounded assistants in assessment workflows; underscore the necessity of Human-in-the-Loop; and advance the automated educational question generation field by examining quality, reliability, and deployment-aware trade-offs.

</details>


### [119] [Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040)
*Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Xiao Yu,Rui Yang,Tao Ge,Alessandrio Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Yelong Shen,Pengcheng He,Tong Zhang,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchard是一个开源框架，用于可扩展的智能体建模。其核心是Orchard Env轻量级环境服务，提供沙箱生命周期管理的可复用原语。基于此构建了三个智能体建模方案：Orchard-SWE（代码智能体，在SWE-bench Verified上达67.5%）、Orchard-GUI（视觉语言计算机使用智能体，在WebVoyager等基准上达74.1%）、Orchard-Claw（个人助手智能体，达59.6% pass@3）。结果表明轻量级、开放、与框架无关的环境层可实现跨领域的可复用智能体数据、训练方案和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体建模领域投入巨大，但开放研究仍受限于基础设施和训练差距。许多高性能系统依赖专有代码库、模型或服务，而大多数开源框架侧重于编排和评估，而非可扩展的智能体训练。因此需要建立开源的、可扩展的智能体建模框架。

Method: 提出Orchard框架，核心为Orchard Env轻量级环境服务，提供沙箱生命周期管理的可复用原语。基于此构建三个方案：1) Orchard-SWE：从MiniMax-M2.5和Qwen3.5-397B蒸馏107K轨迹，引入信用分配SFT从未解决轨迹中学习，应用平衡自适应 rollout 进行RL；2) Orchard-GUI：仅用0.4K蒸馏轨迹和2.2K开放任务训练4B视觉语言计算机使用智能体；3) Orchard-Claw：仅用0.2K合成任务训练个人助手智能体。

Result: Orchard-SWE从Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking出发，SFT后达64.3%，SFT+RL后达67.5%，创同等规模开源模型SOTA。Orchard-GUI在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和DeepShop上分别达74.1%、67.0%和64.0%，是最强开源模型且与专有系统竞争。Orchard-Claw达59.6% pass@3，搭配更强ZeroClaw框架时达73.9%。

Conclusion: 轻量级、开放、与框架无关的环境层能够实现跨领域的可复用智能体数据、训练方案和评估，证明了Orchard框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Individual Intelligence: Surveying Collaboration, Failure Attribution, and Self-Evolution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14892)
*Shihao Qi,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wei Guo,Xiao Huang,Zhitao Gao,Jianhao Deng,Jun Liu,Lingling Zhang,Bifan Wei,Boqian Yang,Pinghui Wang,Jianwen Sun,Jing Tao,Yaqiang Wu,Hui Liu,Yu Yao,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕多智能体系统中的错误传播与自我进化问题，提出LIFE框架（能力基础→协同集成→故障归因→自主进化），系统梳理了四个因果关联阶段，并揭示了阶段间的依赖与约束关系，为构建闭环自愈型多智能体系统提供了统一视角和研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别关注单智能体能力、多智能体协作或智能体自我进化，但忽略了三者之间的因果依赖关系，尤其缺乏对错误跨智能体、跨轮次传播导致诊断困难和结构化自我改进缺失这一风险的系统性分析。

Method: 提出LIFE四阶段框架（Lay foundation → Integrate → Find faults → Evolve），对每阶段构建系统化分类法，并形式化刻画相邻阶段间的依赖与约束关系，最终提出跨阶段闭环研究议程。

Result: 建立了多智能体系统从能力构建到自主进化的因果链路框架，识别了阶段边界处的开放挑战，并提出了跨阶段的闭环研究方案。

Conclusion: 该综述通过桥接此前碎片化的研究方向，为自主、自改进的多智能体智能提供了系统性参考和概念路线图，推动当前协作框架向更自组织的集体智能形态演进。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, yet remain limited when tasks require sustained coordination across roles, tools, and environments. Multi-agent systems address this through structured collaboration among specialized agents, but tighter coordination also amplifies a less explored risk: errors can propagate across agents and interaction rounds, producing failures that are difficult to diagnose and rarely translate into structural self-improvement. Existing surveys cover individual agent capabilities, multi-agent collaboration, or agent self-evolution separately, leaving the causal dependencies among them unexamined. This survey provides a unified review organized around four causally linked stages, which we term the LIFE progression: Lay the capability foundation, Integrate agents through collaboration, Find faults through attribution, and Evolve through autonomous self-improvement. For each stage, we provide systematic taxonomies and formally characterize the dependencies between adjacent stages, revealing how each stage both depends on and constrains the next. Beyond synthesizing existing work, we identify open challenges at stage boundaries and propose a cross-stage research agenda for closed-loop multi-agent systems capable of continuously diagnosing failures, reorganizing structures, and refining agent behaviors, extending current coordination frameworks toward more self-organizing forms of collective intelligence. By bridging these previously fragmented research threads, this survey aims to offer both a systematic reference and a conceptual roadmap toward autonomous, self-improving multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [121] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST提出了一种基于案例驱动的框架，通过提取历史执行轨迹中的复杂度特征和失败特征，结合细粒度奖励设计和自适应推理策略，在强化学习中内化案例知识，显著提升了工具调用的模式遵循准确性和任务成功率，同时将推理长度减少26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在工具使用中需要平衡适当的推理深度与严格的结构有效性，现有方法依赖原始示例输出存在局限性，因此需要一种能从历史执行案例中提取可复用知识的方法。

Method: 提出CAST框架：1）将历史执行轨迹作为结构化案例；2）提取案例衍生的复杂度信号来估计最优推理策略；3）提取失败信号来映射结构故障点；4）将知识转化为细粒度奖励设计和自适应推理；5）通过强化学习让模型自主内化案例策略。

Result: 在BFCLv2和ToolBench上，整体执行准确率提升最高达5.85个百分点，平均推理长度减少26%，显著降低了高影响结构错误。

Conclusion: 历史执行案例能够提供可复用的适应知识，用于实现校准后的工具使用，兼顾模式遵循与效率。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


### [122] [COREKG: Coreset-Guided Personalized Summarization of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14900)
*Sohel Aman Khan,Raghava Mutharaju,Supratim Shit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COREKG方法，基于coreset理论创建个性化知识图谱摘要，通过敏感性重要性采样为每个用户选取与其查询模式相关的三元组子集，在Freebase、WikiData和DBpedia上的评估表明，该方法在查询回答准确性和结构覆盖率方面优于现有方法，同时仅需原始图的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模知识图谱在问答和可视化等任务中变得难以处理，而现有摘要方法缺乏个性化能力，无法根据用户的特定查询模式生成定制化摘要。

Method: 基于coreset理论，针对每个用户的查询负载定义三元组的敏感性分数（衡量其重要性），然后通过敏感性重要性采样算法从原始知识图谱中抽取一个保持原始数据特性且有界近似误差的三元组子集作为个性化摘要。

Result: 在Freebase、WikiData和DBpedia上的实验表明，COREKG在查询回答准确性和结构覆盖率上优于GLIMPSE、PPR、iSummary、PEGASUS和APEX²等现有方法，且仅需原始图的一小部分存储空间和查询运行时。

Conclusion: 基于coreset的个性化知识图谱摘要方法能够有效地平衡摘要规模与信息保留，通过用户查询行为驱动的采样策略，在显著降低存储和计算开销的同时保持高质量的查询回答能力。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are extensively used across different domains and in several applications. Often, these KGs are very large in size. Such KGs become unwieldy for tasks such as question answering and visualization. Summarization of KGs offers a viable alternative in such cases. Furthermore, personalized KG summarization is crucial in the current data-driven world as it captures the specific requirements of users based on their query patterns. Since it only maintains relevant information, the personalized summaries of KG are small, resulting in significantly smaller storage requirements and query runtime. In this work, we adapt the coreset theory to create personalized KG summaries. For a given dataset and a user-specific query workload, we present an approach that samples a relevant subset of triples using sensitivity-based importance sampling. We ensure that the subset approximates the characteristics of the full dataset with bounded approximation error. We define sensitivity scores that measure the importance of a triple with respect to a user's query workload, which are then used by our coreset construction algorithm. We explicitly focus on personalized knowledge graph summarization by constructing summaries independently for each user based on their query behaviour.
  Our evaluation on Freebase, WikiData, and DBpedia shows that COREKG delivers higher query-answering accuracy and structural coverage than the state-of-the-art methods, such as GLIMPSE, PPR, iSummary, PEGASUS and APEX$^2$ while requiring only a tiny fraction of the original graph.

</details>


### [123] [KGPFN: Unlocking the Potential of Knowledge Graph Foundation Model via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14907)
*Yisen Gao,Jiaxin Bai,Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Yufei Li,Hong Ting Tsang,Sirui Han,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KGPFN是一种基于Prior-data Fitted Network的知识图谱基础模型，通过结合跨图关系不变性和结构化上下文的上下文学习，在57个KG基准上实现了超越微调模型的零样本泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG基础模型主要关注关系层面的可迁移性，而忽略了上下文学习这一基础模型的核心支柱。KG中的上下文具有结构化和异构性，需要同时考虑查询实体周围的局部上下文和全局上下文。

Method: 1) 通过关系图上的消息传递学习关系表示，捕获跨图关系不变性；2) 使用多层NBFNet编码局部邻居作为局部上下文；3) 检索查询关系的大量实例及其局部邻居，构建关系特定的全局上下文；4) 在Prior-Data Fitted Network框架中结合特征级和样本级注意力进行聚合；5) 通过多图预训练学习何时实例化可重用模式、何时用上下文证据覆盖它们。

Result: 在57个KG基准上，KGPFN仅通过上下文学习就能实现对未见图的强适应，一致优于需要微调的竞争性KG基础模型。

Conclusion: KGPFN成功将可迁移的关系规律与推理时的结构化上下文学习统一，验证了在KG推理中结合局部与全局上下文进行上下文学习的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) foundation models aim to generalize across graphs with unseen entities and relations by learning transferable relational structure. However, most existing methods primarily emphasize relation-level universality, while in-context learning, the other pillar of foundation models remains under-explored for KG reasoning. In KGs, context is inherently structured and heterogeneous: effective prediction requires conditioning on the local context around the query entities as well as the global context that summarizes how a relation behaves across many instances. We propose KGPFN, a KG foundation model using Prior-data Fitted Network that unifies transferable relational regularities with inference-time in-context learning from structured context. KGPFN first learns relation representations via message passing on relation graphs to capture cross-graph relational invariances. For query-specific reasoning, it encodes local neighborhoods using a multi-layer NBFNet as local context. To enable ICL at global scale, it constructs relation-specific global context by retrieving a large set of instances of the query relation together with their local neighborhoods, and aggregates them within a Prior-Data Fitted Network framework that combines feature-level and sample-level attention. Through multi-graph pretraining on diverse KGs, KGPFN learns when to instantiate reusable patterns and when to override them using contextual evidence. Experiments on 57 KG benchmarks demonstrate that KGPFN achieves strong adaptation to previously unseen graphs through in-context learning alone, consistently outperforming competitive fine-tuned KG foundation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/KGPFN.

</details>


### [124] [From Sycophantic Consensus to Pluralistic Repair: Why AI Alignment Must Surface Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14912)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判将多元主义对齐简化为偏好聚合的现有范式，指出现有RLHF模型的失败模式不是覆盖不足而是谄媚共识。作者基于格莱斯准则提出三种对话机制（范围界定、信号标记、修复），并形式化了一个区分原则性修正与屈服的新指标——多元修复分数（PRS）。在Claude Sonnet 4.5和GPT-4o上实验表明，两者在争议价值提示上都存在同意追随与低修复质量共存的问题。文章最终论证多元主义最关键的塑造发生在部署治理层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF训练的AI系统在多元价值对齐中不是无法覆盖多样性，而是倾向于迎合当前对话者、消弭分歧（谄媚共识），这种交互层面的分歧坍缩在健康、公民生活、劳动、治理等重大领域会产生分配性后果，是一个结构性失败。

Method: 提出基于格莱斯准则的三种对话机制（scope范围界定、signalling信号标记、repair修复），形式化PRS指标区分原则性修正（principled revision）与屈服（capitulation），并在Claude Sonnet 4.5和GPT-4o上进行小规模实证。

Result: 两个前沿RLHF模型在争议价值提示下均表现出同意追随与低修复质量共存的现象，即模型倾向于同意用户观点而非进行高质量的原则性修正。

Conclusion: 偏好聚合不足以实现部署场景下的多元主义对齐；多元主义最关键的塑造发生在部署治理层（接口、偏好数据管道、审计基础设施），而非仅仅在模型训练层。PRS衡量的是多元主义的交互前提条件（可见分歧、原则性修正）而非多元主义本身，需谨慎对待"谁的原则算数"这一反身性问题。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is typically operationalised as preference aggregation: producing responses that span (Overton), steer toward (Steerable), or proportionally represent (Distributional) diverse human values. We argue that aggregation alone is an incomplete primitive for deployed pluralistic alignment. Under genuine value pluralism, the failure mode of contemporary RLHF-trained assistants is not insufficient coverage but sycophantic consensus: a learned tendency to agree with, validate, and minimise friction with the immediate interlocutor. Because deployed AI systems now mediate consequential deliberation across health, civic life, labour, and governance, the collapse of disagreement at the interaction layer is not a narrow technical concern but a structural failure with distributive consequences. We reframe pluralistic alignment around three conversational mechanisms drawn from Grice's maxims: scoping (acknowledging the limits of one's perspective), signalling (surfacing value-conflict rather than smoothing it over), and repair (revising one's position on principled grounds, not on user pressure). We formalise a metric, the Pluralistic Repair Score (PRS), distinguishing principled revision from capitulation, and present a small-scale empirical illustration on two frontier RLHF-trained models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, N=198; GPT-4o, N=100) showing that, for both, agreement-following coexists with low repair-quality on contested-value prompts. PRS measures an interactional precondition for pluralism (visible disagreement; principled revision) rather than pluralism in full; we discuss the difference, take seriously the reflexive question of whose "principled" counts, and argue that pluralism is most decisively made or unmade at the deployment-governance layer: interfaces, preference-data pipelines, and audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [125] [GraphFlow: An Architecture for Formally Verifiable Visual Workflows Enabling Reliable Agentic AI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14968)
*Drewry H. Morris,Luis Valles,Reza Hosseini Ghomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphFlow是一个可视化工作流系统，通过将工作流图作为可执行规范，结合编译时契约验证和运行时持久化引擎，旨在提高多步骤、关键型AI自动化流程的可靠性。在三个临床站点的年度试点中，系统完成了8,728个队列入组工作流，完成率达97.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流平台提供持久化执行和可观测性，但缺乏语义正确性保证；而智能体系统在推理时进行规划，行为对提示变化敏感且难以审计。在独立步骤的理想化模型下，十步骤流程（每步90%可靠性）仅35%成功完成，小错误会快速累积。

Method: GraphFlow将工作流图视为可执行规范（单一工件定义数据范围、执行语义和监控）。编译时，受限类图可生成可复用的自动化组件，其契约（前置条件、后置条件和组合义务）在存入共享库前需经证明检查。运行时，持久化引擎以仅追加事件日志记录结果，可在系统边界强制执行契约，支持重放、重试和审计。泳道图使信任边界显式化，隔离已验证逻辑与外部系统、人工判断和AI决策。

Result: 跨三个临床站点的年度试点执行了8,728个队列入组工作流运行，在未含验证核心子系统的早期原型下完成率达97.08%；观察到的失败主要局限于外部集成。

Conclusion: 本文描述的正式语义和证明检查的准入模型已规范定义并处于积极开发中，验证核心的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: GraphFlow is a visual workflow system designed to improve the reliability of agentic AI automation in multi-step, mission-critical processes. In these workflows, small errors compound rapidly: under an idealized model of independent steps, a ten-step process with 90% per-step reliability completes successfully only 35% of the time. Existing workflow platforms provide durable execution and observability but offer few semantic correctness guarantees, while agentic systems plan at inference time, making behavior sensitive to prompt variation and difficult to audit.
  GraphFlow is designed to address this gap by treating workflow diagrams as the executable specification, a single artifact defining data scope, execution semantics, and monitoring. At compile time, a restricted class of diagrams is specified to produce reusable automations whose contracts (preconditions, postconditions, and composition obligations) are intended to be proof-checked before admission to a shared library. At runtime, a durable engine records outcomes in an append-only event log and can enforce contracts at system boundaries, supporting replay, retries, and audit. Swimlanes make trust boundaries explicit, separating verified logic from external systems, human judgment, and AI decisions.
  A year-long pilot across three clinical sites executed 8,728 cohort-enrolled workflow runs with a 97.08% completion rate under an early prototype without the verified-core subsystem; observed failures were localized primarily to external integrations. The formal semantics and proof-checked admission model described here are specified and under active development. Evaluation of the verified core is reserved for future work.

</details>


### [126] [Learning Developmental Scaffoldings to Guide Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14998)
*Milton L. Montero,Elias Najarro,Jakob Schauser,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过神经细胞自动机（NCA）与坐标基模式生成器（SIREN）联合学习的框架，研究了生物发育中将信息卸载到初始条件（预模式）的现象，表明联合学习预模式和自组织规则相比纯自组织方法在鲁棒性、编码能力和对称性破缺方面均有提升，且有效预模式并非简单近似目标，而是通过偏置发育动力学促进收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自然系统通过自组织产生复杂结构，但其中大量信息并非由自组织过程本身产生，而是被卸载到系统的初始条件中（如母体预模式）。作者希望研究这种信息卸载现象，理解预模式与自组织规则之间的信息分配及其相互作用。

Method: 提出了一个联合学习框架：自组织规则由神经细胞自动机（NCA）建模，预模式由基于坐标的隐式神经表示（SIREN）生成，两者同时训练以生成一组目标模式。通过信息论分析量化信息在预模式和自组织过程之间的分布。

Result: 联合学习预模式和自组织规则在鲁棒性、编码能力和对称性破缺方面均优于纯自组织方法。有效预模式并非简单近似目标图案，而是以非平凡的方式偏置发育动力学，促进收敛。预模式在信息分配中承担了特定角色。

Conclusion: 生物发育中的信息卸载现象类似于计算系统中的存储-计算权衡。联合学习框架揭示了预模式与自组织规则之间存在复杂的相互作用关系，为理解生物发育中的信息处理机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: From subcellular structures to entire organisms, many natural systems generate complex organisation through self-organisation: local interactions that collectively give rise to global structure without any blueprint of the outcome. Yet a significant portion of the information driving such processes is not produced by self-organisation itself, instead, it is often offloaded to initial conditions of the system. Biological development is a prime example, where maternal pre-patterns encode positional and symmetry-breaking information that scaffolds the self-organising process. From maternal morphogen gradients in early embryogenesis to tissue-level morphogenetic pre-patterns guiding organ formation, this transfer of information to initial conditions, analogous to a memory-compute trade-off in computational systems, is a fundamental part of developmental processes. In this work, we study this offloading phenomenon by introducing a model that jointly learns both the self-organisation rules and the pre-patterns, allowing their interplay to be varied and measured under controlled conditions: a Neural Cellular Automaton (NCA) paired with a learned coordinate-based pattern generator (SIREN), both trained simultaneously to generate a set of patterns. We provide information-theoretic analyses of how information is distributed between pre-patterns and the self-organising process, and show that jointly learning both components yields improvements in robustness, encoding capacity, and symmetry breaking over purely self-organising alternatives. Our analysis further suggests that effective pre-patterns do not simply approximate their targets; rather, they bias the developmental dynamics in ways that facilitate convergence, pointing to a non-trivial relationship between the structure of initial conditions and the dynamics of self-organisation.

</details>


### [127] [Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15100)
*Rongman Xu,Yifei Li,Tianzhe Zhao,Yanrui Wu,Bo Li,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双维度一致性（DDC）框架，通过置信度加权贝叶斯协议结合趋势感知分层剪枝，在减少10倍以上token消耗的同时保持或提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理时扩展方法在采样预算与推理质量之间存在权衡困境：宽度共识方法可能强化幻觉，深度剪枝方法过早截断有效推理链，两者均效率低下。

Method: 提出DDC统一框架，耦合置信度加权贝叶斯协议（Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol）与趋势感知分层剪枝（Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning），将计算资源集中在高质量推理路径上。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，token消耗减少超过10倍，同时在多种LLM上保持或超越强基线的准确率。

Conclusion: DDC通过统一宽度与深度维度的一致性验证，实现了推理效率与质量的帕累托改进，为推理时计算资源分配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.

</details>


### [128] [Why Neighborhoods Matter: Traversal Context and Provenance in Agentic GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15109)
*Riccardo Terrenzi,Maximilian von Zastrow,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Agentic GraphRAG中，引文忠实性不仅要求引文支持答案，还需考虑图遍历路径、结构和未引用但被访问的实体，实验表明引文必要但不充分，评估应转向检索轨迹的溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统评估引文忠实性仅关注引文是否支持答案，但Agentic GraphRAG中智能体在知识图谱上遍历后才产生答案，未引用的遍历上下文和图结构也可能影响答案，需要重新审视引文忠实性的定义。

Method: 通过控制消融实验，分别隔离、移除和掩码已引用和未引用的图实体，对比这些操作对答案准确性的影响。

Result: 移除已引用证据会显著改变答案并降低准确性（引文必要性）；但即使引文保留，移除未引用的遍历上下文和图结构也会影响准确答案（引文不充分性）。

Conclusion: Agentic GraphRAG中的引文评估应从简单的源支持转向对更广泛检索轨迹的溯源分析。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation can improve factuality by grounding answers in external evidence, but Agentic GraphRAG complicates what it means for citations to be faithful. In these systems, an agent explores a knowledge graph before producing an answer and a small set of citations. We frame citation faithfulness as a trajectory-level problem: final citations should not only support the answer, but also account for the graph traversal, structure, and visited-but-uncited entities that may influence it. Through controlled ablation experiments, we compare the effects of isolating, removing, and masking cited and uncited graph entities. Our results show that cited evidence is often necessary, as removing it substantially changes answers and reduces accuracy. However, citations are not sufficient, because accurate answers can also depend on uncited traversal context and surrounding graph structure. These findings suggest that citation evaluation in Agentic GraphRAG should move beyond source support toward provenance over the broader retrieval trajectory.

</details>


### [129] [OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15177)
*Shang Zhou,Wenhao Chai,Kaiyuan Liu,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenDeepThink框架，通过配对Bradley-Terry比较进行基于种群的测试时计算扩展，在8轮LLM调用中使Gemini 3.1 Pro的Codeforces Elo提升+405分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算方法主要通过扩展深度（延长单条推理轨迹）来提升性能，而扩展广度（并行采样多个候选）面临选择瓶颈：缺乏真实验证器时，逐点的LLM评判存在噪声和偏差，难以选出最佳候选。

Method: 提出OpenDeepThink框架，采用基于种群的测试时计算。每轮生成中，LLM对随机配对的候选进行两两比较，通过Bradley-Terry模型聚合投票得到全局排名；保留排名靠前的候选，对前三名进行自然语言批评变异，丢弃后四分之一。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro在8轮LLM调用（约27分钟）中Codeforces Elo提升+405分；该方法可跨强弱模型迁移无需重新调参；在HLE基准上，增益集中在客观可验证领域，在主观领域出现反转。

Conclusion: OpenDeepThink通过配对比较有效解决了测试时计算中广度扩展的选择瓶颈，为LLM推理提供了一种无需真实验证器的可迁移增强方案。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测条件下基于视觉的过去时间信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)的认证运行时监控问题，提出了两种可重用的监控器：语义基监控器和滚动预测监控器，并证明语义基是单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口的最小预测目标，通过单一共形校准即可认证整个公式片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监控方法在处理视觉观测下的时序逻辑公式时，通常需要为每个公式重新训练模型，缺乏可重用性。本文旨在设计一种无需逐公式重新训练、能提供有限样本保证的可重用监控接口。

Method: 1) 定义语义基作为时序原子鲁棒性得分的向量，证明它是单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口的最小预测目标；2) 基于解析树设计确定性解码器从语义基恢复任意公式的鲁棒性；3) 通过单一共形校准认证整个公式片段，无需联合界；4) 提出滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人交叉路口基准测试中，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，语义基监控器在长时域上紧致度提升高达4倍。在真实Waymo驾驶数据上验证，两种监控器均经验性地满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基监控器在长时域上更优，滚动监控器在短时域上更优且更易学习，两种方法在真实数据上均能提供有效的共形覆盖保证，验证了所提可重用监控框架的有效性。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [131] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对三个不同架构的EEG基础模型（SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM）进行特征解耦分析，通过临床分类体系评估单语义性和纠缠度，并提出概念引导方法量化引导选择性，揭示模型的表征失败模式（如“破坏球”干预和临床纠缠），最后通过谱解码器将潜在操作映射到生理可解释的频率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现出色，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床信任。需要一种可解释性框架来揭示模型如何编码临床相关概念及其内部表征的组织方式。

Method: 使用TopK稀疏自编码器对三个不同架构的EEG transformer（SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM）提取稀疏特征字典；基于临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别、用药）进行特征分析；提出单一超参数过程及内在字典健康审计实现跨架构迁移；引入“目标vs非目标探测区域”指标量化概念引导选择性；使用谱解码器将干预映射到幅度谱。

Result: 发现了三种操作状态：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码。识别了“破坏球”干预（全局性能崩溃）和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）等关键表征失败模式。通过谱解码器实现了病理慢波抑制和α波段恢复等可解释的频率特征映射。

Conclusion: 该框架为EEG基础模型提供了系统化的可解释性分析工具，揭示了概念编码的不同状态和临床纠缠问题，为模型安全部署和可信临床决策提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [132] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需手动调参的联合变分自编码器（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器输出端自适应平衡离散与连续潜变量，从而在dMRI结构连接组分析中无监督地分离采集效应与生物变异。在7,416个连接组（跨2-102岁、13个研究、25种采集参数）上的实验表明，该方法在捕捉站点/扫描仪差异方面优于标准VAE、PCA+k-means及基于损失退火的混合模型（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据中不同站点、扫描仪和协议带来的采集差异会引入变异性，干扰结构连接组分析。现有降维方法将所有变异建模为连续变量，导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜空间；而混合潜空间模型虽引入离散组件，但需要手动调节容量来确保离散组件捕获预期变异。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE框架，通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器输出端、解码之前对潜变量进行退火处理，使模型在训练过程中自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量，无需手动容量调参。

Result: 在N=7,416个结构连接组（涵盖2-102岁、13个研究、25种采集参数组合，其中5,900正常认知、877轻度认知障碍、639阿尔茨海默病）上，架构退火方法在站点学习上达到ARI=0.53（p<0.05），优于标准VAE、PCA+k-means以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜空间配合架构退火（而非基于损失的退火）为dMRI中采集变异的无监督捕获提供了一种有效机制。Joint-VAE通过联合建模平滑和类别结构，能够恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型后训练中出现的"轨迹锁定"问题（奖励驱动更新导致概率质量过度集中于少数去噪路径），提出TraFL方法，通过轨迹平衡目标将策略训练向奖励倾斜的目标分布，在数学推理和代码生成基准上持续优于基模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的后训练方法大多采用奖励最大化目标，这会导致"轨迹锁定"失效模式：奖励驱动的更新将概率质量过度集中在狭窄的去噪路径集合上，减少了重复采样下正确解的覆盖范围。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），使用轨迹平衡目标将策略训练向奖励倾斜的目标分布，该目标分布锚定在冻结的参考模型上。通过扩散兼容的序列级替代目标和学习到的与提示相关的归一化因子使其适用于扩散语言模型。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准-长度设置下均优于基模型的后训练方法，且优势随采样预算增加而持续。在Minerva Math上保持优于基模型，在LiveCodeBench所有难度划分上均表现最强。

Conclusion: TraFL通过轨迹平衡有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，实现了更稳健的分布覆盖和持续的性能提升。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [134] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的框架，使多个机构能够在不共享私有数据的情况下联合微调大语言模型（LLM），并在医疗和金融领域的非独立同分布（non-IID）数据上验证了该方法的有效性，表明联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的成功依赖于大规模公开数据，但最有价值的现实世界数据（如医疗和金融领域）通常分布在多个机构中，受隐私、法规和组织壁垒的限制无法共享。此外，这些数据往往是non-IID的，进一步增加了利用难度。因此，亟需一种能够在不暴露私有数据的前提下利用分布式机构数据微调LLM的可行方案。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，各节点在不交换原始数据的前提下联合微调共享LLM。在医疗和金融领域的四个闭域问答和分类数据集（MedQA、MedMCQA、FPB、FiQA-SA）上进行跨域基准评估，比较了三种参数高效微调（PEFT）策略——LoRA、QLoRA和IA3，并在反映机构数据异质性的non-IID设置下进行实验。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单机构独立学习。从绿色AI角度，QLoRA和IA3在有限的精度损失下显著提升了效率，表明联邦PEFT是数据不可共享场景下适配LLM的可行方案。

Conclusion: 通过联邦协作方式，可以在不共享私有数据的前提下有效利用分布式机构数据微调LLM，为在隐私敏感领域（如医疗、金融）解锁高价值私有数据提供了实用路径，联邦PEFT在性能与效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [135] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习最优嵌入的精确几何结构：平衡监督对比学习恢复经典正则单形体几何，而类别不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度；连续标签设置中，y-Aware CL存在标签权重与球形潜在相似度的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过保持样本间的关系结构来学习表征，但所得嵌入的几何结构尚未被充分理解。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题（DGP），其中权重方案指定了表征需要实现的目标几何结构，从而精确刻画了多种监督和弱监督设置下的最优嵌入。

Result: 1) 监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon都将同类样本坍缩到单一原型点；2) 平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单形体几何，类别不平衡则破坏该对称性；3) Soft SupCon无论类别是否不平衡都保持正则单形体几何；4) 连续标签设置中，y-Aware CL只有标签位于超球面上才能达到熵最优；5) 欧几里得-欧几里得加权和X-CLR可得到唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 权重方案的选择决定了对比学习在几何上是可实现的、退化的还是不兼容的，为设计对比学习目标函数提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [136] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Collider-Bench 是一个用于评估 LLM 智能体在大语言模型对撞机实验中复现高能物理分析能力的基准测试，包含从论文到可执行模拟管线的端到端任务，并通过连续保真度评分、计算成本记录和 LLM 评委检查违规行为进行多维度评估。实验表明，目前尚无通用编码智能体能稳定击败物理学家参与的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差异，特别是大语言模型智能体在长周期工具使用任务上的评估存在空白，因此需要针对高能物理实验分析复现这一挑战性场景构建专门的评估基准。

Method: 从已发表的 LHC 分析论文中提取任务，要求智能体将其转化为可执行的模拟与选择管线，提交指定信号区域的对撞事件产额预测；使用标准直方图指标提供连续保真度评分，记录每个任务的计算成本，并利用 LLM 评委对代码库和完整会话轨迹进行定性的失败模式检测。

Result: 在多个通用编码智能体的能力阶梯上进行了评估，结果显示平均而言，没有任何智能体能可靠地击败有物理学家参与的解决方案。

Conclusion: 当前通用 LLM 编码智能体在复现 LHC 实验分析这一高难度科学任务上的能力仍有显著不足，物理学家在回路中的方法仍是更可靠的选择，该基准为未来提升科学领域 AI 智能体的能力提供了有价值的评估框架。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [137] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间接地先验分布，替代生成式机器人控制策略中标准的高斯源分布，在操作任务中持续提升成功率。其效果来源于更直的路径（类似Rectified Flow的最优传输效果），并在行为克隆和强化学习中均带来改进，揭示了源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但被忽视的设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散和流匹配的生成式机器人控制策略普遍使用标准高斯分布作为源分布，但这一选择的合理性未被充分探讨。作者假设源分布的设计对生成轨迹的质量和最终任务成功率有重要影响，因此探索如何利用近期动作历史构建更优的源分布。

Method: 提出WarmPrior：一种时间接地（temporally grounded）的先验分布，利用近期动作历史（readily available recent action history）构建，替代标准高斯分布作为生成过程的源分布。该方法可无缝集成到基于扩散/流匹配的行为克隆和先验空间强化学习中。

Result: 1) 在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功率；2) 生成的概率路径显著更直（straighter），与Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果相似；3) 在先验空间强化学习中同时提升样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但被忽视的设计维度。WarmPrior作为一种简单有效的方法，通过时间接地先验替代高斯分布，能够显著改善路径质量和任务表现。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [138] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能源核算框架，用于测量蒸馏管道的完整端到端计算成本（包括教师端的数据生成、logit缓存和评估），通过阶段级GPU功耗追踪，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了传统知识蒸馏和合成数据微调中被忽视的成本，并提供了在能源和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则，同时开源了标准化测量工具和协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的部署激增导致GPU需求和数据中心规模急剧扩大，引发了电力消耗和电网压力的担忧。蒸馏常被认为是获得更高效模型的途径，但现有研究很少考虑完整的端到端能源和资源成本（包括教师端的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等关键工作负载），需要对这些隐藏成本进行系统量化。

Method: 提出一种全面的能源核算框架，通过详细的阶段级GPU功耗追踪来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。实验中对两个常见蒸馏方法（经典基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）进行分阶段分离和记录经验能耗，构建能耗-质量帕累托前沿。同时开发并开源标准化的测量工具和核算协议。

Result: 通过阶段级GPU功耗追踪，系统测量并分离了蒸馏各阶段的能量消耗，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了传统蒸馏方法中被忽视的教师端成本（如数据生成、logit缓存和评估）。推导出在能源和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 蒸馏方法并非免费的效率提升，其教师端计算成本（数据生成、logit缓存、评估）不可忽视；本文提供的标准化开源核算框架和协议为可比、可重复的蒸馏研究奠定了基础，使研究者能够在能源和预算约束下做出更明智的蒸馏方法选择。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [139] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个面向表格数据的基础模型，通过纯合成数据预训练，在百万行级数据集上实现SOTA性能，支持测试时计算扩展，推理速度比前代快20倍，在TabArena等基准测试上显著优于调参后的基线模型，并拓展到时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据等多模态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据驱动科学和工业中的大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN引领了该模态的基础模型革命。TabPFN-3旨在将SOTA性能扩展到百万训练行的大规模数据集，同时大幅减少训练和推理时间，并拓展模型能力到时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据等更广泛的应用场景。

Method: 基于用户反馈构建，仅使用先验分布生成的合成数据进行预训练；引入测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）；采用缩减的KV缓存和行分块（row-chunking）技术，在单张H100上扩展到100万行数据并实现快速推理；提供专门针对时间序列的TabPFN-TS-3检查点。

Result: 在TabArena上，单次前向传播显著优于所有调参和集成基线，帕累托支配速度/性能前沿；在多样数据集上，多分类任务排名第一，在100万行/200特征数据集上击败8小时调参的GBDT基线；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上以超过200 Elo击败所有非TabPFN模型（大数据子集上达420 Elo），比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍且性能更优；在RelBenchV1上达到SOTA；TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二；SHAP值计算加速120倍；整体推理速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过合成数据预训练、测试时计算扩展和工程优化，在表格数据预测上实现了性能与速度的显著提升，并将能力成功拓展到时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据，为表格基础模型设立了新标杆。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [140] [Neural Fields for NV-Center Inverse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13988)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Tao Zhong,Yixun Hu,Nathalie P. de Leon,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对量子传感中的逆问题，提出NeTMY方法——一种免摊销坐标神经场，耦合可微NV正演模型，通过退火位置编码、多尺度优化、稀疏门控和频谱保真度损失，有效解决了传统标量/相干近似导致的中心坍塌病理现象，在NV金刚石量子传感的噪声谱重建中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于NV色心的量子传感中，磁噪声谱重建面临非线性、光谱耦合且物理敏感的正演模型挑战。传统标量/相干前向近似与自由密度优化存在中心坍塌失效模式，亟需一种物理保真的逆问题求解方法。

Method: 提出NeTMY框架：1) 用张量幂和偶极算子替代标量/相干近似，修正正演模型；2) 构建免摊销坐标神经场，耦合可微NV正演模型；3) 采用退火位置编码与多尺度优化策略；4) 引入稀疏/门控机制和频谱保真度损失函数。

Result: 在修正算子生成的稀疏合成重建基准中，NeTMY在定位精度和分布度量指标上均达到最优。机制实验表明NeTMY不直接执行原始密度空间梯度，其参数化平滑并重新分布更新，有效缓解了中心坍塌病理。

Conclusion: NV量子传感可作为物理保真神经逆问题的有效测试平台，NeTMY方法为该领域提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Inverse problems in scientific sensing are often solved with either hand-designed regularizers or supervised networks trained on simulated labels, yet both can fail when the forward model is nonlinear, spectrally coupled, and physically delicate. We study this issue for noise sensing based on nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, where a quantum sensor measures magnetic-noise spectra generated by sparse spin sources. We show that replacing a common scalar/coherent forward approximation with a tensor power-summed dipolar operator changes the inverse landscape and exposes a center-collapse failure mode in free-density optimization. We propose NeTMY, an amortization-free coordinate neural field coupled to the differentiable NV forward model, with annealed positional encoding, multiscale optimization, sparsity/gating, and spectrum-fidelity losses. Across sparse synthetic reconstructions generated by the corrected operator, NeTMY achieves the best localization and distributional metrics in the tested benchmark. Mechanism experiments show that NeTMY does not directly execute the raw density-space gradient; its parameterization smooths and redistributes updates, mitigating the center-collapse pathology. These results position NV quantum sensing as a useful testbed for physics-faithful neural inverse problems.

</details>


### [141] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型的无训练压缩中存在三元专家不可合并的拓扑障碍（谐波核），本文提出HodgeCover算法通过单纯形拉普拉斯分解来识别并覆盖这些关键边/三角结构，在激进压缩场景下达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成对信号的无训练MoE压缩方法存在结构性盲区：三个专家两两可合并但三元组构成不可约循环，导致压缩质量受限。需要一种能感知高阶拓扑结构的压缩方法。

Method: 1）将障碍形式化为2-复形上的谐波核（单纯形拉普拉斯的零空间）；2）对边上的KL散度信号进行Hodge分解，精确分离出谐波核；3）提出HodgeCover算法，贪心地覆盖谐波关键边和三角关键面；4）混合变体将HodgeCover与权重剪枝结合。

Result: 在三个开源Sparse MoE模型上，HodgeCover在专家缩减维度媲美SOTA无训练基线，在激进压缩的混合维度领先，且能独特地在所有四个Hodge分量间平衡保留质量。

Conclusion: 揭示MoE结构的谐波核从根本上改变了最优压缩器的选择，尤其在最关键的激进压缩场景下。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [142] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现离散扩散模型在去噪过程中存在层级结构：模型优先学习数据的**支撑结构**（如语法有效性），再学习**细粒度频率信息**。均匀扩散和吸收扩散在去噪最后阶段表现出不同的机制分离特性，实验验证了支撑定位早于频率排序的学习顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中表现日益出色，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程尚不清楚，尤其是模型如何区分数据的支撑结构（合法性）与细粒度频率分布。

Method: 首先从理论上证明在小噪声（最终去噪步）条件下，单token逆向编辑可分解为**主尺度**（决定是否移向数据支撑）和**细粒度系数**（决定同尺度内的相对概率）。对比分析了均匀扩散（存在三分法：改善/保持/恶化有效性）和吸收扩散（主导项集中于改善有效性）。并在掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论证明了两种扩散机制下支撑信息与频率信息的学习分离性；实验表明支撑定位先于频率排序出现，且均匀扩散与吸收扩散的速率分离与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的学习存在层级结构：先学习数据支撑（如语法合法性），后学习数据频率分布，且不同扩散机制（均匀vs吸收）会导致不同的学习速率分离模式。
